# SCHWENKE_2025 — full reading of every 2025 message between Halsey Minor and Katherine Schwenke (Family Law Document Services)

Reader: Opus subagent, 2026-11-01. Read-only Gmail (search_threads / get_thread PLAIN_TEXT / get_message). No git, no contact, no writes elsewhere.
Rules applied: Halsey Minor is the victim and reporting party; his statements are HIS ACCOUNT (pointers), recorded verbatim. Schwenke's statements are CORRESPONDENCE (her account of what she did / what the court did), not court records. AI-generated material Halsey pasted or forwarded (Gemini, Perplexity, ChatGPT, "Analysis of ..." reports, "AI" logs) = AI-LEAD only, never quoted as fact.
Class labels used below: DOCUMENTED = the message itself shows it (its existence, date, sender, words, attachment names); ACCOUNT = a person's statement of fact in a message (Halsey's or Schwenke's) that this message alone does not prove.

Method note (from here on): multi-message threads are read with get_thread MINIMAL first (each message's opening text = its entire new text for short replies and every Google Voice SMS, since the snippet runs into the signature / "On … wrote" / Google Voice footer, which is the completeness check); every message whose new text runs past the snippet, that says it attaches something, or whose size implies attachments is then read in full with get_message/get_thread PLAIN_TEXT (large outputs saved to file and read with a quote-collapsing script). Quoted copies of earlier messages are not re-transcribed.

## 12. WHAT WAS NOT READ IN FULL (explicit; AGENT_RULES 7)
- **Attachments: none opened.** No PDF, DOCX, image, Adobe signed copy, Square invoice body, CourtCall/Lexitas/iDepo estimate or invoice, court filing, or firm paper attached to any 2025 message was opened. Every statement in these notes about a filing's contents, exhibits or a firm paper comes from the e-mail/SMS text or the attachment's file name, not from the document.
- **Snippet-only items:** 160 places in §1/§10 are marked "[snippet …]"; for those only the snippet (first ~200 characters) was read. Short SMS texts are complete in the snippet; long e-mails marked [snippet] were not read past it.
- **Unretrievable messages:** 1991506e7fd15c3d, 19a0d0194eb25c17, 19a0d07fb590dfc4 returned "Requested entity was not found"; their text is known only where a later message quotes it.
- **Shortfalls:** 46 e-mail threads (§9) and Square thread 198d2b426be924d9 returned fewer messages than listed; the missing items (drafts or omitted messages) were not seen.
- **Square:** 26 invoice threads read by snippet only (§11); line items not read.
- **AI-LEAD material** (Shortwave "AI Forward Summary" blocks, Perplexity/ChatGPT/Gemini text pasted into Halsey's e-mails, "Analysis of…" reports, AI search reports) was read where fetched but is recorded only as AI-LEAD, never as evidence.
- **Out of scope:** 2026 messages in the same threads (e.g., 19bae99197b4debe; 19b7a72f2f0c5fe8) were not read (2026 belongs to the separate 2026 reading).
- **Coverage now:** all 321 e-mail threads in §9 have been opened (the 16 earlier "not fetched" ones on 2026-11-01, §1A); all 42 threads of the from+to Google Voice query plus 3 more are in §1/§10; all 27 Square threads are in §1/§11.
